0000191C          .findachannel ;new FOR QTMv1.28!
0000191C                    ;
0000191C                    ; updated behaviour v1.44
0000191C                    ; is any channel is currently playing an effect
0000191C                    ; if so, the highest channel playing an effect is our backup channel, otherwise the highest channel is backup

00002390          .tmr_setlinetime 
00002390                    ;
00002390                    ; R9  s=speed
00002390                    ; R10 c=CIA tempo
00002390                    ;
00002390                    ; equation; time per line=10s/4c (*65536 for accuracy)
00002390                    ;
00002390 E92D4003           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R1,R14}
00002394 E3A0090A           MOV     R0,#&28000
00002398 E0000099           MUL     R0,R9,R0

000023A0 EBFFFFFE           BL      r0_div_r1               ;do (10s<<16)/4c - result in r0<<16
000023A4 E1A08000           MOV     R8,R0
000023A8 E8BD8003           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R1,PC}
000023AC          
000023AC          
000023AC                    ; R12=song_data
000023AC                    ; R11=address of current word
000023AC                    ; R10=CIA tempo (32-255)
000023AC                    ; R9=speed (1-31)
000023AC                    ; R8=time per line = (10*speed/4*cia tempo)*65536
000023AC                    ; R7=time so far
000023AC                    ; R6=channel-1 (0-4/6/8)
000023AC                    ; R5=current word
000023AC                    ; R4=sequence
000023AC                    ; R3=event
000023AC

00002514          .tmr_E_effects ;r0=effect number (always &E!!!), r1=FX data, r9=channel
00002514 E1A00221           MOV     R0,R1,LSR#4             ;r0 now has E effect number
00002518 E201100F           AND     R1,R1,#&0F              ;r1 now has data nibble for E effect
0000251C E59C2024           LDR     R2,[R12,#E_FXflags]     ;load E effect status flags
00002520 E3A03001           MOV     R3,#1
00002524 E1120013           TST     R2,R3,LSLR0             ;is this effect's flag set on?
00002528 0AFFFFF8           BEQ     tmr_badFX               ;if not, skip it...
0000252C          
0000252C          
0000252C E3500006           CMP     R0,#&6
00002530 0AFFFFFE           BEQ     tmr_pattloop            ;E6 - pattern loop
00002534 E350000E           CMP     R0,#&E
00002538 0AFFFFFE           BEQ     tmr_pattdelay           ;EE - pattern delay
0000253C EAFFFFF3           B       tmr_badFX
00002540          
00002540

00002558 E8BD807F           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R6,PC}

000025B0 07C20006           STREQB  R0,[R2,R6]
000025B4 08BD807F           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R6,PC}         ;if 'set loop', load event and put it in loop start
000025B8          
000025B8          
000025B8 E7D30006           LDRB    R0,[R3,R6]
000025BC E2500001           SUBS    R0,R0,#1                ;load current loop value, sub 1
000025C0 41B00001           MOVMIS  R0,R1                   ; if now <0 then it was 0 (so not in a loop) so set new
000025C4 A7C30006           STRGEB  R0,[R3,R6]              ; if new value used or (value-1)>=0, then store new value
000025C8 08BD807F           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R6,PC}         ; if (value-1)=0 then loop has ended, so exit
000025CC          
000025CC

000028D8                    ;
000028D8                    ; New real VU code 18/4/2001
000028D8                    ;
000028D8                    ; This code calculates the VU levels by examining the samples QTM is currently playing
000028D8                    ; rather than scanning the RISC OS DMA buffer data and calculating the levels from there.
000028D8                    ;
000028D8                    ; The new routine produces identical output to scanning the DMA buffer (on 8bit sound comps)
000028D8                    ; however now works on RiscPCs with 16bit sound, which do not have an 8bit sound buffer!
000028D8                    ;
000028D8          
000028D8          
000028D8          .realVUs  ;new realVUs code (gives correct result on 16bit sound)
000028D8 E92D1FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12}                   ;r14 stored already
000028DC E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0
000028E0 E5CC0018           STRB    R0,[R12,#vuflags]
000028E4          
000028E4          
000028E4 E1A0900C           MOV     R9,R12                          ;R9=start of QTM vars block (R12 usually)
000028E8 E2898D0B           ADD     R8,R9,#(DMA_blocks-song_data)   ;R8=channel DMA data
000028EC E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
000028F0          
000028F0          
000028F0 E5992028           LDR     R2,[R9,#vuheights]              ;old heights for channels 1-4
000028F4 E3A03000           MOV     R3,#0                           ;new heights for channels 1-4, 5-8
000028F8 E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0
000028FC          .realvu_channelloop 
000028FC EBFFFFFE           BL      realvu_chanctrl                 ;returns R0=new average vu height for this channel
00002900 E20210FF           AND     R1,R2,#&FF                      ;old average vu height for this channel
00002904 E0811000           ADD     R1,R1,R0
00002908 E18330A1           ORR     R3,R3,R1,LSR#1                  ;put averaged value in R3
0000290C E1A03463           MOV     R3,R3,ROR#8
00002910 E1A02462           MOV     R2,R2,ROR#8
00002914          
00002914          
00002914 E2888018           ADD     R8,R8,#24
00002918 E2866001           ADD     R6,R6,#1
0000291C E3360004           TEQ     R6,#4
00002920 05893028           STREQ   R3,[R9,#vuheights]              ;store new heights for channels 1-4
00002924 03A03000           MOVEQ   R3,#0
00002928 0599202C           LDREQ   R2,[R9,#vuheights58]            ;old heights for channels 5-8
0000292C E156000B           CMP     R6,R11
00002930 BAFFFFF1           BLT     realvu_channelloop
00002934          
00002934          
00002934 E589302C           STR     R3,[R9,#vuheights58]            ;store new heights for channels 5-8
00002938 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
0000293C          
0000293C          
0000293C          .realvu_chanctrl ;r6=channel-1 (0-7)
0000293C E92D5FFE           STMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,R14}       ;
00002940 E898003F           LDMIA   R8,{R0-R5}              ;r0=end addr, r1=-ve offset, r2=pitch, r3=vol/flag, r4=rlen
00002944 E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)
00002948 C3A07003           MOVGT   R7,#3                   ; <put min loop len in r7>
0000294C C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)
00002950 CAFFFFFE           BGT     realvu_blankchannel     ;if offset>-4096 and 3>repeat len, then exit
00002954          
00002954          
00002954 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;v1.45 - huge bug - can't believe address=0 worked for so long!
00002958 13520000           CMPNE   R2,#0
0000295C DAFFFFFE           BLE     realvu_blankchannel     ;pitch is 0, so sample is blank
00002960          
00002960          
00002960          .realvu_chanrunning 
00002960 E1B03C03           MOVS    R3,R3,LSL#24            ;if DMA_sampleflag set, CS because &FF shifted off
00002964 32897FF1           ADDCC   R7,R9,#(musicvoltable-song_data)
00002968 22897D73           ADDCS   R7,R9,#(samplevoltable-song_data)
0000296C E7D73C23           LDRB    R3,[R7,R3,LSR#24]       ;load Arc scaled volume
00002970          
00002970          
00002970 E35300FE           CMP     R3,#254                 ;if volume is too low
00002974 AAFFFFFE           BGE     realvu_blankchannel     ;channel is blank....
00002978          
00002978          
00002978 E3520B01           CMP     R2,#1024                        ;
0000297C B2897C0B           ADDLT   R7,R9,#(arcperiods-song_data)
00002980 B7972102           LDRLT   R2,[R7,R2,LSL#2]                ;load Arc pitch increment
00002984 ABFFFFFE           BLGE    amigatoarcpitch_1024            ;for high periods, use calculation - returns r2=arc pitch
00002988          
00002988          
00002988 E3550002           CMP     R5,#2                   ;is this sample a looping sample?
0000298C B3A05002           MOVLT   R5,#2                   ;if not, make repoff=2, so when sample ends, last 2 bytes repeat
00002990          
00002990          
00002990                    OPT opt%
00002990 E1A00000           MOV R0,R0
00002994 E1A00000           MOV R0,R0
00002998                    FNlong_adr("  ",10,loglintab)
00002998 E3A0B000           MOV     R11,#0
0000299C E3A0C080           MOV     R12,#128                ;get average real vu height from 128 bytes of DMA data
000029A0          
000029A0          
000029A0          .realvu_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
000029A0 E7D0E641           LDRB    R14,[R0,R1,ASR#12]      ;load a byte of sample data
000029A4 E05EE003           SUBS    R14,R14,R3              ;scale for volume
000029A8 C3CEE001           BICGT   R14,R14,#%00000001      ;make +ve log (=even no.s)
000029AC C7DAE00E           LDRGTB  R14,[R10,R14]           ;load +ve linear byte (0-127)
000029B0 C08BB00E           ADDGT   R11,R11,R14             ;if>0 accumulate
000029B4 E0911082           ADDS    R1,R1,R2,LSL#1          ;add pitch*2, have we ended? (offset becomes GE)?
000029B8 A0511605           SUBGES  R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if so, get back to repeat start (and check offset now ok)
000029BC A0411605           SUBGE   R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if offset still -ve, get back to repeat start
000029C0          
000029C0          
000029C0 E25CC002           SUBS    R12,R12,#2
000029C4 CAFFFFF5           BGT     realvu_sampleloop       ;if fake DMA ok and still playing, then loop back
000029C8          
000029C8          
000029C8                    ; now R11 has 64*bytes (0 to 127), so need to divide by 128 (= >>7)to get value 0-63
000029C8          
000029C8          
000029C8 E1A003AB           MOV     R0,R11,LSR#7            ;return R0=average vu level (0-64) for this DMA fill
000029CC          
000029CC          
000029CC                    ;ADD     R0,R0,R0,LSR#2          ;**old** linear scale-up so the vu fills 0-64 better
000029CC                    ;AND     R0,R0,#63               ; (..this on does 0-80, then clips down to 0-63!)
000029CC          
000029CC          
000029CC E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;better log sq'd scale-up routine (T's ;-)
000029D0 E1A01000           MOV     R1,R0                   ;
000029D4 E0000091           MUL     R0,R1,R0                ;
000029D8 E1A00320           MOV     R0,R0,LSR#6             ;
000029DC E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;this does 0-64 on a log (sq'd) curve, so low vols are more amp'd
000029E0 E1500000           CMP     R0,R0 ; Clear V
000029E4 E8BD9FFE           LDMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,PC}
000029E8          
000029E8          
000029E8          .realvu_blankchannel 
000029E8 E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0
000029EC E8BD9FFE           LDMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,PC}        ;exit with R0=vu height for this channel
000029F0          
000029F0          
000029F0                    ;
000029F0                    ; SWI QTM_DMABuffer and fake DMA Buffer creation routine for 16bit sound RiscPCs
000029F0                    ;
000029F0          
000029F0          
000029F0          .swi_qtmdmabuffer 
000029F0 E59C0078           LDR     R0,[R12,#fake8bitDMA]
000029F4 E3300000           TEQ     R0,#0
000029F8 059C00BC           LDREQ   R0,[R12,#DMABuffer]
000029FC 01A0F00E           MOVEQ   PC,R14
00002A00          
00002A00          
00002A00 E92D4000           STMFD   R13!,{R14}
00002A04 EBFFFFFE           BL      calcscope               ;update fake DMA block for 16bit-sound RiscPCs
00002A08 E8BD8000           LDMFD   R13!,{PC}
00002A0C          
00002A0C          
00002A0C          .calcscope ;called by SWI QTM_DMABuffer on 16bit sound RiscPCs
00002A0C E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}               ;to generate a fake 8bit sound DMA buffer for progs use!
00002A10 E1A0900C           MOV     R9,R12                          ;R9=start of QTM vars block (R12 usually)
00002A14 E599C078           LDR     R12,[R9,#fake8bitDMA]           ;R12=fake DMA address
00002A18          
00002A18          
00002A18 E2898D0B           ADD     R8,R9,#(DMA_blocks-song_data)   ;R8=channel DMA data
00002A1C E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
00002A20 E08CA20B           ADD     R10,R12,R11,LSL#4
00002A24 E08AA30B           ADD     R10,R10,R11,LSL#6               ;v1.45
00002A28 E08AA38B           ADD     R10,R10,R11,LSL#7               ;***** fake DMA is always 208 bytes * no. of channels
00002A2C          
00002A2C          
00002A2C E1A0600B           MOV     R6,R11
00002A30          .calcscope_channelloop 
00002A30 EBFFFFFE           BL      calcscope_chanctrl
00002A34          
00002A34          
00002A34 E2888018           ADD     R8,R8,#24
00002A38 E28CC001           ADD     R12,R12,#1
00002A3C E2566001           SUBS    R6,R6,#1
00002A40 CAFFFFFA           BGT     calcscope_channelloop
00002A44 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
00002A48          
00002A48          
00002A48          .calcscope_chanctrl 
00002A48 E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}       ;
00002A4C E898003F           LDMIA   R8,{R0-R5}              ;r0=end addr, r1=-ve offset, r2=pitch, r3=vol/flag, r4=rlen
00002A50 E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)
00002A54 C3A07003           MOVGT   R7,#3                   ; <put min loop len in r7>
00002A58 C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)
00002A5C CAFFFFFE           BGT     calcscope_blankchannel  ;if offset>-4096 and 3>repeat len, then exit
00002A60          
00002A60          
00002A60 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;v1.45 - huge bug - can't believe address=0 worked for so long!
00002A64 13520000           CMPNE   R2,#0
00002A68 DAFFFFFE           BLE     calcscope_blankchannel
00002A6C          
00002A6C          
00002A6C          .calcscope_chanrunning 
00002A6C E1B03C03           MOVS    R3,R3,LSL#24            ;if DMA_sampleflag set, CS because &FF shifted off
00002A70 32897FF1           ADDCC   R7,R9,#(musicvoltable-song_data)
00002A74 22897D73           ADDCS   R7,R9,#(samplevoltable-song_data)
00002A78 E7D73C23           LDRB    R3,[R7,R3,LSR#24]       ;load Arc scaled volume
00002A7C          
00002A7C          
00002A7C E35300FE           CMP     R3,#254                 ;if volume is too low
00002A80 AAFFFFFE           BGE     calcscope_blankchannel  ;go to clear the channel...
00002A84          
00002A84          
00002A84          
00002A84          
00002A84 E3520B01           CMP     R2,#1024                        ;
00002A88 B2897C0B           ADDLT   R7,R9,#(arcperiods-song_data)
00002A8C B7972102           LDRLT   R2,[R7,R2,LSL#2]                ;load Arc pitch increment
00002A90 ABFFFFFE           BLGE    amigatoarcpitch_1024            ;for high periods, use calculation - returns r2=arc pitch
00002A94          
00002A94          
00002A94 E3550002           CMP     R5,#2                   ;is this sample a looping sample?
00002A98 B3A05002           MOVLT   R5,#2                   ;if not, make repoff=2, so when sample ends, last 2 bytes repeat
00002A9C          
00002A9C          
00002A9C          .calcscope_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
00002A9C E7D06641           LDRB    R6,[R0,R1,ASR#12]       ;load a byte of sample data
00002AA0 E0566003           SUBS    R6,R6,R3                ;scale for volume
00002AA4 43A06000           MOVMI   R6,#0                   ;reset to 0 if vol too low
00002AA8 E6CC600B           STRB    R6,[R12],R11            ;store in fake DMA block
00002AAC E0911002           ADDS    R1,R1,R2                ;have we ended (offset becomes GE)?
00002AB0 A0411605           SUBGE   R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if so, get back to repeat start
00002AB4          
00002AB4          
00002AB4 E15C000A           CMP     R12,R10                 ;check fake DMA end...
00002AB8 BAFFFFF7           BLT     calcscope_sampleloop    ;if fake DMA ok and still playing, then loop back
00002ABC E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
00002AC0          
00002AC0          
00002AC0          .calcscope_blankchannel 
00002AC0 E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0                   ;
00002AC4          .calcscope_clear ;
00002AC4 E6CC600B           STRB    R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002AC8 E15C000A           CMP     R12,R10                 ;
00002ACC B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002AD0 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;
00002AD4 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002AD8 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;
00002ADC B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002AE0 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;only using 4 blankings per loop...
00002AE4 BAFFFFF6           BLT     calcscope_clear         ;...cleared rest of channel
00002AE8 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}        ;exit

000036C4                    .CIAhandler                    ;r12 = song_data on entry
000036C4 E92D0003           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R1}           ;stack r0,r1
000036C8 E89C0003           LDMIA   R12,{R0-R1}            ;r0=CIA delay, r1=CIA counter
000036CC E2511801           SUBS    R1,R1,#1<<16           ;take 1 lump of accurate time off CIA counter
000036D0 D0811000           ADDLE   R1,R1,R0               ;if counter<=0 then add total CIA delay to it
000036D4 E58C1004           STR     R1,[R12,#CIAcounter]   ;store CIA counter
000036D8 E8BD0003           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R1}           ;reload r0,r1
000036DC C1A0F00E           MOVGT   PC,R14                 ;if CIA counter didn't run out, then exit

000036EC D5DC0008           LDRLEB  R0,[R12,#speed]         ;if counter<=0 then reload it with the speed
000036F0 E5CC0009           STRB    R0,[R12,#counter]       ;store counter

000041DC E201331F           AND     R3,R1,#&1F<<26          ;get pos in bits 26-30 (bit 31 is sign, so clear it)
000041E0 E3110C01           TST     R1,#1<<8
000041E4 1AFFFFFE           BNE     vib_rampdown            ;
000041E8 E3110C02           TST     R1,#2<<8
000041EC 13A030FF           MOVNE   R3,#255
000041F0                    ;ADDEQ   R4,R12,#(vibratotable-song_data) AND &FF00
000041F0                    ;ADDEQ   R4,R4,#(vibratotable-song_data) AND &00FF
000041F0 024F4008           ADREQ   R4,vibratotable_new
000041F4 07D43D23           LDREQB  R3,[R4,R3,LSR#26]
000041F8          .vib_set  
000041F8 E201400F           AND     R4,R1,#&0F
000041FC E0030394           MUL     R3,R4,R3
00004200 E21124FF           ANDS    R2,R1,#&FF000000        ;26-31 pos (+sign) (bits 24 & 25 are 0, because shifted 2)
00004204 A08003A3           ADDGE   R0,R0,R3,LSR#7
00004208 404003A3           SUBMI   R0,R0,R3,LSR#7
0000420C

000042B4          .do_tremolo ; ***** 07 - tremolo *****
000042B4 E5DB000F           LDRB    R0,[R11,#currentvol]
000042B8 E59B1028           LDR     R1,[R11,#tremolo_speed] ;0-3 depth, 4-7 speed, 8-9 wave, 10=no reset, 26-30 pos, 31 sign
000042BC E201331F           AND     R3,R1,#&1F<<26          ;=&7C<<24 ;get pos in bits 26-30 (bit 31 is sign, so clear it)
000042C0 E3110C03           TST     R1,#%11<<8
000042C4                    ;ADDEQ   R4,R12,#(vibratotable-song_data) AND &FF00
000042C4                    ;ADDEQ   R4,R4,#(vibratotable-song_data) AND &00FF
000042C4 024F4038           ADREQ   R4,vibratotable_new
000042C8 07D43D23           LDREQB  R3,[R4,R3,LSR#26]       ;not ASR, as b31 just used to flag add/sub value
000042CC 0AFFFFFE           BEQ     trem_set                ;PT prioritises 0/4 (sine)
000042D0          
000042D0          
000042D0 E3110C01           TST R1,#%01<<8                  ;...then 1/5 (sawtooth)
000042D4 1AFFFFFE           BNE trem_rampdown
000042D8 E3A030FF           MOV R3,#255 ;1 seems to work with 4mat test track  ;...then 2/6 (sqr, and 3/7 treated as 2/6)
000042DC          .trem_set 
000042DC E201400F           AND     R4,R1,#&0F              ;depth
000042E0 E0030394           MUL     R3,R4,R3                ;depth*value
000042E4 E21124FF           ANDS    R2,R1,#&FF000000        ;R2=bits 26-30 pos (+b31 sign) (b24 & b25=0, because pos 26-30)
000042E8 50800323           ADDPL   R0,R0,R3,LSR#6
000042EC 40500323           SUBMIS  R0,R0,R3,LSR#6
000042F0 43A00000           MOVMI   R0,#0
000042F4 E3500040           CMP     R0,#64
000042F8 C3A00040           MOVGT   R0,#64
000042FC          
000042FC          
000042FC E5CA000C           STRB    R0,[R10,#DMA_volume]
00004300          
00004300          
00004300 E20110F0           AND     R1,R1,#&F0       ;=speed in bits %11110000
00004304 E0822B01           ADD     R2,R2,R1,LSL#(24-2) ;add speed to pos (bits 26-30 + b31 sign)
00004308 E1A02C42           MOV     R2,R2,ASR#24     ;keep sign, move to bits 0-7 (v1.49 fix - vibrato was fixed in 1.46a!)
0000430C E5CB202B           STRB    R2,[R11,#tremolo_pos]
00004310 EAFFFDD8           B       nextFX
00004314

000054E4          .loglintab ;this is precalculated and loaded from a data file at assembly
000055E4                    OPT opt%
000055E4          
000055E4                    ;
000055E4                    ; Ultra compact pattern-shuffle, (c) STEP Harrison 2013
000055E4                    ;
000055E4                    ; uses precalculated table (64 bytes) and 80 bytes of code = 144 bytes total
000055E4                    ; in order to reshuffle 2048 bytes of pattern data using no additional memory
000055E4                    ;
000055E4                    ; input pattern format c1-4 events 0,1,2...63 then c5-8 events 0,1,2...63
000055E4                    ; output pattern format c1-8 events 0,1,2,3....61,62,63
000055E4                    ;
000055E4                    ; shuffle example input  0,1,2,3,4,5.....124,125,126,127
000055E4                    ;         example output 0,64,1,65,2,66,3,67,4....125,62,126,63,127
000055E4                    ;
000055E4                    ; shuffle table calculated by hand/using spreadsheet...is there a better way?
000055E4                    ;
000055E4          
000055E4          .shufflepattern ;reorder StarTrekker patters into FastTracker format
000055E4 E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}   ;R0=start addr of 8 channel pattern to reshuffe
000055E8 E24F2008           ADR     R2,shuffletable     ;(alternate one offset from table, one from R1)
000055EC          
000055EC                    ;if table byte=0, skip, if table byte=255 end
000055EC E2801B01           ADD     R1,R0,#64*16        ;(64 to 127, use inbetween table bytes)
000055F0 E1A03000           MOV     R3,R0               ;output address
000055F4          .shuffleloop 
000055F4 E4D24001           LDRB    R4,[R2],#1          ;load table byte
000055F8 E3340000           TEQ     R4,#0               ;0=skip
000055FC 02833010           ADDEQ   R3,R3,#1*16         ;get to next event position
00005600 0AFFFFFE           BEQ     skipthisshuffle
00005604          
00005604          
00005604 E33400FF           TEQ     R4,#255             ;255=exit
00005608 08BD9FFF           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
0000560C          
0000560C          
0000560C E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;load 4 channels from current event
00005610 E0804204           ADD     R4,R0,R4,LSL#4
00005614 E8941E00           LDMIA   R4,{R9,R10,R11,R12} ;load 4 channels from destination event
00005618 E88401E0           STMIA   R4,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
0000561C E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12};swap them round and increment to next event
00005620          .skipthisshuffle 
00005620 E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;...do the next event using R1 as swap address
00005624 E8911E00           LDMIA   R1,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
00005628 E8A101E0           STMIA   R1!,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
0000562C E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
00005630 EAFFFFEF           B       shuffleloop         ;keep going until end of table
00005634          
00005634

00005674                    FNassm_initsong ;QTM_Song
00005674                    OPT opt%
00005674          
00005674                    ; on entry
00005674          
00005674                    ;          R0          \  sample end address            - sample end address
00005674                    ;          R1           } -ve sample offset             - -ve sample offset
00005674                    ;          R2           } (amiga type) pitch            - sample pitch increment (Arc)
00005674                    ;          R3           } linear volume (0-64)          - sample volume (0-255)
00005674                    ;          R4           } -ve repeat offset             - -ve repeat offset
00005674                    ;          R5          /                                - load/store sample byte
00005674                    ;          R6      -ve if channel handler changed       - channel counter (0->channel-1)
00005674                    ;          R7      <unknown>                            -
00005674                    ;          R8      uS <? - unknown>                     - ptr to 'DMA_blocks'
00005674                    ;          R9      ptr to initblock                     - ptr to 'song_data' (usually R12)
00005674                    ;          R10     DMA end                              - DMA end
00005674                    ;          R11     DMA gap                              - DMA gap
00005674                    ;          R12     DMA start                            - DMA ptr
00005674                    ;          R13     stack                                - stack
00005674                    ;          R14     exit                                 - end
00005674

00002390          .tmr_setlinetime 
00002390                    ;
00002390                    ; R9  s=speed
00002390                    ; R10 c=CIA tempo
00002390                    ;
00002390                    ; equation; time per line=10s/4c (*65536 for accuracy)
00002390                    ;
00002390 E92D4003           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R1,R14}
00002394 E3A0090A           MOV     R0,#&28000
00002398 E0000099           MUL     R0,R9,R0
0000239C E1A0100A           MOV     R1,R10
000023A0 EB0010DE           BL      r0_div_r1               ;do (10s<<16)/4c - result in r0<<16
000023A4 E1A08000           MOV     R8,R0
000023A8 E8BD8003           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R1,PC}
000023AC          
000023AC          
000023AC                    ; R12=song_data
000023AC                    ; R11=address of current word
000023AC                    ; R10=CIA tempo (32-255)
000023AC                    ; R9=speed (1-31)
000023AC                    ; R8=time per line = (10*speed/4*cia tempo)*65536
000023AC                    ; R7=time so far
000023AC                    ; R6=channel-1 (0-4/6/8)
000023AC                    ; R5=current word
000023AC                    ; R4=sequence
000023AC                    ; R3=event
000023AC          
000023AC          
000023AC          .calctime

000023C0 E3A07000           MOV     R7,#0                   ;R7=total time
000023C4 E3A04000           MOV     R4,#0

000023CC          .tmr_mainloop

000023D8

000023F4 E28BB084           ADD     R11,R11,#132            ;add 132, to get to start of patterns, and put in r11
000023F8          
000023F8          
000023F8 E59C607C           LDR     R6,[R12,#patternlen]    ;v1.44 support for 8CHN and 6CHN
000023FC E02BB690           MLA     R11,R0,R6,R11           ;add current patt*patternlen to pattern start
00002400 E1A06326           MOV     R6,R6,LSR#6             ;patternlen/64=eventlen (16/24/32 for 4, 8FLT/6/8)
00002404 E02BB693           MLA     R11,R3,R6,R11           ;add on current event*event length (no longer just 16)
00002408          
00002408          
00002408 E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0                   ;r6=channel-1
0000240C          .tmr_dochannels 
0000240C                    ;TEQ     R6,#4                   ;v1.45 update, no longer need this...
0000240C                    ;LDREQB  R5,[R12,#songtype]      ;if chan 4 we must be > 4 chans (0-3) so either 6 or 8
0000240C                    ;TEQEQ   R5,#&10                 ;if also 'FLT8' type, need to get to next patt, add 1024

00002410 E205080F           AND     R0,R5,#&000F0000        ; \
00002414 E350080B           CMP        R0,#&000B0000        ;  \_ if effects B,D,E,F
00002418 1350080D           CMPNE      R0,#&000D0000        ;  /  then deal with them...
0000241C AB00002C           BLGE    tmr_checkoutFX          ; /
00002420          
00002420          
00002420 E5DC0047           LDRB    R0,[R12,#songchans]

00002428 E1560000           CMP     R6,R0                   ;is <4 or 8 (no more channels to do)?
0000242C BAFFFFF6           BLT     tmr_dochannels          ;if not, then loop back
00002430          
00002430          
00002430 E3590000           CMP     R9,#0                   ;is speed 0, after doing all channels?
00002434 0A000018           BEQ     tmr_gottime             ;if so, we've got the time!
00002438          
00002438          
00002438          .tmr_checkpattdel 
00002438 E5DC041C           LDRB    R0,[R12,#tm_patterndelay]
0000243C E2500001           SUBS    R0,R0,#1
00002440 A5CC041C           STRGEB  R0,[R12,#tm_patterndelay];take off pattern delay (if running)
00002444          
00002444          
00002444 E59C3418           LDR     R3,[R12,#tm_currentevent];load r3 with sequence (16-23) event (0-7)
00002448 D2833001           ADDLE   R3,R3,#1                ;add 1 to event counter
0000244C E1A04823           MOV     R4,R3,LSR#16
00002450          
00002450          
00002450 E5DC041D           LDRB    R0,[R12,#tm_breakflags] ;load the break flag byte
00002454 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;is it 0?
00002458 1A000015           BNE     tmr_checkjumps          ;if so, go to jump checking routine...
0000245C          .tmr_checkevent

00002460 05CC3418           STREQB  R3,[R12,#tm_currentevent];if so, so store event counter
00002464 0A000009           BEQ     tmr_nextline
00002468          
00002468          
00002468 E3A03000           MOV     R3,#0                   ;set event counter to 0, because we are at a new pattern
0000246C          .tmr_brokepattern ;  **-{pattern break code entry}-**
0000246C E2844001           ADD     R4,R4,#1                ;add 1 to current sequence
00002470          .tmr_jumped2seq ; **-{jump to sequence code entry}-** <<< unused now >>>
00002470 E5DC001E           LDRB    R0,[R12,#maxsequence]
00002474 E1540000           CMP     R4,R0                   ;is current sequence > max sequence?
00002478 AA000007           BGE     tmr_gottime
0000247C E5DC241F           LDRB    R2,[R12,#tm_lastsequence];load last sequence num
00002480 E1540002           CMP     R4,R2                   ;is current sequence < last sequence?

00002490          .tmr_nextline 
00002490 E0977008           ADDS    R7,R7,R8
00002494 5AFFFFCC           BPL     tmr_mainloop
00002498 EAFFFFA6           B       tmr_crashed             ;if number has got too large and become negative, bad...
0000249C          
0000249C          
0000249C          .tmr_gottime 
0000249C D0877008           ADDLE   R7,R7,R8                ;v1.45 previously forgot to add time for the final line!!!
000024A0 E1A023A7           MOV     R2,R7,LSR#7             ;
000024A4 E3A00064           MOV     R0,#100                 ;v1.45 return value in centiseconds, for improved accuracy

000024B0 E8BD8FFB           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R1,R3-R11,PC}  ;                                     ...and exit

000028D8          
000028D8                    ;
000028D8                    ; New real VU code 18/4/2001
000028D8                    ;
000028D8                    ; This code calculates the VU levels by examining the samples QTM is currently playing
000028D8                    ; rather than scanning the RISC OS DMA buffer data and calculating the levels from there.
000028D8                    ;
000028D8                    ; The new routine produces identical output to scanning the DMA buffer (on 8bit sound comps)
000028D8                    ; however now works on RiscPCs with 16bit sound, which do not have an 8bit sound buffer!
000028D8                    ;
000028D8          
000028D8          
000028D8          .realVUs  ;new realVUs code (gives correct result on 16bit sound)
000028D8 E92D1FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12}                   ;r14 stored already
000028DC E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0
000028E0 E5CC0018           STRB    R0,[R12,#vuflags]
000028E4          
000028E4          
000028E4 E1A0900C           MOV     R9,R12                          ;R9=start of QTM vars block (R12 usually)
000028E8 E2898D0B           ADD     R8,R9,#(DMA_blocks-song_data)   ;R8=channel DMA data
000028EC E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
000028F0          
000028F0          
000028F0 E5992028           LDR     R2,[R9,#vuheights]              ;old heights for channels 1-4
000028F4 E3A03000           MOV     R3,#0                           ;new heights for channels 1-4, 5-8
000028F8 E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0
000028FC          .realvu_channelloop 
000028FC EB00000E           BL      realvu_chanctrl                 ;returns R0=new average vu height for this channel
00002900 E20210FF           AND     R1,R2,#&FF                      ;old average vu height for this channel
00002904 E0811000           ADD     R1,R1,R0
00002908 E18330A1           ORR     R3,R3,R1,LSR#1                  ;put averaged value in R3
0000290C E1A03463           MOV     R3,R3,ROR#8
00002910 E1A02462           MOV     R2,R2,ROR#8
00002914          
00002914          
00002914 E2888018           ADD     R8,R8,#24
00002918 E2866001           ADD     R6,R6,#1
0000291C E3360004           TEQ     R6,#4
00002920 05893028           STREQ   R3,[R9,#vuheights]              ;store new heights for channels 1-4
00002924 03A03000           MOVEQ   R3,#0
00002928 0599202C           LDREQ   R2,[R9,#vuheights58]            ;old heights for channels 5-8
0000292C E156000B           CMP     R6,R11
00002930 BAFFFFF1           BLT     realvu_channelloop
00002934          
00002934          
00002934 E589302C           STR     R3,[R9,#vuheights58]            ;store new heights for channels 5-8
00002938 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
0000293C          
0000293C          
0000293C          .realvu_chanctrl ;r6=channel-1 (0-7)
0000293C E92D5FFE           STMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,R14}       ;
00002940 E898003F           LDMIA   R8,{R0-R5}              ;r0=end addr, r1=-ve offset, r2=pitch, r3=vol/flag, r4=rlen
00002944 E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)
00002948 C3A07003           MOVGT   R7,#3                   ; <put min loop len in r7>
0000294C C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)
00002950 CA000024           BGT     realvu_blankchannel     ;if offset>-4096 and 3>repeat len, then exit
00002954          
00002954          
00002954 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;v1.45 - huge bug - can't believe address=0 worked for so long!
00002958 13520000           CMPNE   R2,#0
0000295C DA000021           BLE     realvu_blankchannel     ;pitch is 0, so sample is blank
00002960          
00002960          
00002960          .realvu_chanrunning 
00002960 E1B03C03           MOVS    R3,R3,LSL#24            ;if DMA_sampleflag set, CS because &FF shifted off
00002964 32897FF1           ADDCC   R7,R9,#(musicvoltable-song_data)
00002968 22897D73           ADDCS   R7,R9,#(samplevoltable-song_data)
0000296C E7D73C23           LDRB    R3,[R7,R3,LSR#24]       ;load Arc scaled volume
00002970          
00002970          
00002970 E35300FE           CMP     R3,#254                 ;if volume is too low
00002974 AA00001B           BGE     realvu_blankchannel     ;channel is blank....
00002978          
00002978          
00002978 E3520B01           CMP     R2,#1024                        ;
0000297C B2897C0B           ADDLT   R7,R9,#(arcperiods-song_data)

00002984 AB000DFC           BLGE    amigatoarcpitch_1024            ;for high periods, use calculation - returns r2=arc pitch
00002988          
00002988          
00002988 E3550002           CMP     R5,#2                   ;is this sample a looping sample?
0000298C B3A05002           MOVLT   R5,#2                   ;if not, make repoff=2, so when sample ends, last 2 bytes repeat
00002990          
00002990          
00002990                    OPT opt%

00002990                    OPT opt%
00002990 E28FA04C           EQUD optcode1%
00002994 E28AAC2B           EQUD optcode2%
00002998                    FNlong_adr("  ",10,loglintab)

000029A0          
000029A0          .realvu_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
000029A0 E7D0E641           LDRB    R14,[R0,R1,ASR#12]      ;load a byte of sample data
000029A4 E05EE003           SUBS    R14,R14,R3              ;scale for volume
000029A8 C3CEE001           BICGT   R14,R14,#%00000001      ;make +ve log (=even no.s)
000029AC C7DAE00E           LDRGTB  R14,[R10,R14]           ;load +ve linear byte (0-127)
000029B0 C08BB00E           ADDGT   R11,R11,R14             ;if>0 accumulate
000029B4 E0911082           ADDS    R1,R1,R2,LSL#1          ;add pitch*2, have we ended? (offset becomes GE)?
000029B8 A0511605           SUBGES  R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if so, get back to repeat start (and check offset now ok)
000029BC A0411605           SUBGE   R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if offset still -ve, get back to repeat start
000029C0          
000029C0          
000029C0 E25CC002           SUBS    R12,R12,#2
000029C4 CAFFFFF5           BGT     realvu_sampleloop       ;if fake DMA ok and still playing, then loop back
000029C8          
000029C8          
000029C8                    ; now R11 has 64*bytes (0 to 127), so need to divide by 128 (= >>7)to get value 0-63
000029C8          
000029C8          
000029C8 E1A003AB           MOV     R0,R11,LSR#7            ;return R0=average vu level (0-64) for this DMA fill
000029CC          
000029CC          
000029CC                    ;ADD     R0,R0,R0,LSR#2          ;**old** linear scale-up so the vu fills 0-64 better
000029CC                    ;AND     R0,R0,#63               ; (..this on does 0-80, then clips down to 0-63!)
000029CC          
000029CC          
000029CC E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;better log sq'd scale-up routine (T's ;-)
000029D0 E1A01000           MOV     R1,R0                   ;
000029D4 E0000091           MUL     R0,R1,R0                ;
000029D8 E1A00320           MOV     R0,R0,LSR#6             ;
000029DC E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;this does 0-64 on a log (sq'd) curve, so low vols are more amp'd
000029E0 E1500000           CMP     R0,R0 ; Clear V
000029E4 E8BD9FFE           LDMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,PC}
000029E8          
000029E8

000029EC E8BD9FFE           LDMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,PC}        ;exit with R0=vu height for this channel

00002A9C          .calcscope_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
00002A9C E7D06641           LDRB    R6,[R0,R1,ASR#12]       ;load a byte of sample data

000036C4                    OPT opt%
000036C4                    .CIAhandler                    ;r12 = song_data on entry
000036C4 E92D0003           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R1}           ;stack r0,r1
000036C8 E89C0003           LDMIA   R12,{R0-R1}            ;r0=CIA delay, r1=CIA counter
000036CC E2511801           SUBS    R1,R1,#1<<16           ;take 1 lump of accurate time off CIA counter
000036D0 D0811000           ADDLE   R1,R1,R0               ;if counter<=0 then add total CIA delay to it
000036D4 E58C1004           STR     R1,[R12,#CIAcounter]   ;store CIA counter
000036D8 E8BD0003           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R1}           ;reload r0,r1
000036DC C1A0F00E           MOVGT   PC,R14                 ;if CIA counter didn't run out, then exit
000036E0                    OPT opt%
000036E0          
000036E0          
000036E0          .ControlSong 
000036E0 E92D4FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R11,R14}
000036E4 E5DC0009           LDRB    R0,[R12,#counter]       ;load speed counter
000036E8 E2500001           SUBS    R0,R0,#1                ;take off 1 (can be <0 if speed 0 used to stop song?)
000036EC D5DC0008           LDRLEB  R0,[R12,#speed]         ;if counter<=0 then reload it with the speed
000036F0 E5CC0009           STRB    R0,[R12,#counter]       ;store counter
000036F4 CA0000B2           BGT     doFX                    ;if counter didn't get to 0, then go and do the effects...
000036F8          
000036F8          
000036F8 E5DC000A           LDRB    R0,[R12,#patterndelay]  ;load pattern delay time
000036FC E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;is it 0?
00003700 1A0000AB           BNE     waitdelay               ;if not, then go to the delay effects/count down routine
00003704          
00003704          
00003704          .GetNewNote 
00003704 E5DC101B           LDRB    R1,[R12,#songstopflags] ;now all channels have been checked, test for stop-speed
00003708 E3310000           TEQ     R1,#0
0000370C 1B000184           BLNE    stopflagset             ;returns if we don't stop
00003710          
00003710          
00003710 E28CB00C           ADD     R11,R12,#songaddr       ;r11=addr of song address, then current event vars
00003714 E89B0003           LDMIA   R11,{R0,R1}             ;r0=song address, r1=sequence (16-24) event (0-7)
00003718 E2800FEE           ADD     R0,R0,#952              ;r0=sequence table address
0000371C E7D02821           LDRB    R2,[R0,R1,LSR#16]       ;r2=pattern
00003720 E1811402           ORR     R1,R1,R2,LSL#8          ;r1=sequence (16-24) pattern (8-15) event (0-7)
00003724 E58C1014           STR     R1,[R12,#lastevent]     ;store a copy of the position vars
00003728 E20110FF           AND     R1,R1,#&FF              ;r1=event
0000372C          
0000372C          
0000372C E2808084           ADD     R8,R0,#132              ;add 132 to r0, to get to start of patterns, and put in r10
00003730 E59C007C           LDR     R0,[R12,#patternlen]    ;v1.44 support for 8CHN and 6CHN
00003734 E0288092           MLA     R8,R2,R0,R8             ;add current patt*patternlen to pattern start, gets to this pattern start
00003738 E1A00320           MOV     R0,R0,LSR#6             ;patternlen/64 = event length (16/24/32 for 4, 8 FLT/6/8)
0000373C E0288091           MLA     R8,R1,R0,R8             ;add on current event*event length (no longer just 16)
00003740 E58C8074           STR     R8,[R12,#tempaddrptr]
00003744          
00003744          
00003744 E28CB080           ADD     R11,R12,#(channeldata-song_data)-64 ;r11=addr of channel blocks

00003E30 08BD80FF           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R7,PC}         ;if 'set loop', load event and put it in loop start
00003E34          
00003E34          
00003E34 E5DB0037           LDRB    R0,[R11,#pattloopcount]
00003E38 E2500001           SUBS    R0,R0,#1                ;load current loop value, sub 1
00003E3C 41B00001           MOVMIS  R0,R1                   ; if now <0 then it was 0 (so not in a loop) so set new
00003E40 A5CB0037           STRGEB  R0,[R11,#pattloopcount] ; if new value used or (value-1)>=0, then store new value
00003E44 08BD80FF           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R7,PC}         ; if (value-1)=0 then loop has ended, so exit
00003E48          
00003E48          
00003E48 E5DB0036           LDRB    R0,[R11,#pattloopstart]

00003E58 E5CC001C           STRB    R0,[R12,#breakflags]    ;set break flags
00003E5C E8BD80FF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R7,PC}

00003F98          .updatefunk ;R0=invloopspeed, not called if spd=0 (check above and in do_FX)
00003F98 E92D4007           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R2,R14}
00003F9C E28FE048           ADR     R14,funktable
00003FA0 E7DE0000           LDRB    R0,[R14,R0]
00003FA4 E5DB103B           LDRB    R1,[R11,#invloopoff]
00003FA8 E0811000           ADD     R1,R1,R0
00003FAC E5CB103B           STRB    R1,[R11,#invloopoff]

00003FC4 E59B1010           LDR     R1,[R11,#invaddress]

00003FCC E1510000           CMP     R1,R0

00003FD8 E58B1010           STR     R1,[R11,#invaddress]
00003FDC E5D10000           LDRB    R0,[R1]
00003FE0 E2200001           EOR     R0,R0,#1                ;invert bit 0 = change sign!
00003FE4 E5C10000           STRB    R0,[R1]
00003FE8 E8BD8007           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R2,PC}
00003FEC                    ; - this very rare effect inverts actual sample data (in memory),
00003FEC                    ;   and is the only effect NOT included in QTM<v1.48. Although I
00003FEC                    ;   coded the routine, I never found a song which uses it, until
00003FEC                    ;   4mat provided a test track ;) thanks! Now fully implememnted.
00003FEC                    ;                                                             SH
00003FEC          .funktable 
00003FEC 00                 EQUB   0
00003FED 05                 EQUB   5
00003FEE 06                 EQUB   6
00003FEF 07                 EQUB   7
00003FF0 08                 EQUB   8
00003FF1 0A                 EQUB  10
00003FF2 0B                 EQUB  11
00003FF3 0D                 EQUB  13
00003FF4 10                 EQUB  16
00003FF5 13                 EQUB  19
00003FF6 16                 EQUB  22
00003FF7 1A                 EQUB  26
00003FF8 20                 EQUB  32
00003FF9 2B                 EQUB  43
00003FFA 40                 EQUB  64
00003FFB 80                 EQUB 128
00003FFC                    ALIGN
00003FFC          
00003FFC

000054E4          .loglintab ;this is precalculated and loaded from a data file at assembly
000055E4                    OPT opt%
000055E4          
000055E4                    ;
000055E4                    ; Ultra compact pattern-shuffle, (c) STEP Harrison 2013
000055E4                    ;
000055E4                    ; uses precalculated table (64 bytes) and 80 bytes of code = 144 bytes total
000055E4                    ; in order to reshuffle 2048 bytes of pattern data using no additional memory
000055E4                    ;
000055E4                    ; input pattern format c1-4 events 0,1,2...63 then c5-8 events 0,1,2...63
000055E4                    ; output pattern format c1-8 events 0,1,2,3....61,62,63
000055E4                    ;
000055E4                    ; shuffle example input  0,1,2,3,4,5.....124,125,126,127
000055E4                    ;         example output 0,64,1,65,2,66,3,67,4....125,62,126,63,127
000055E4                    ;
000055E4                    ; shuffle table calculated by hand/using spreadsheet...is there a better way?
000055E4                    ;
000055E4          
000055E4          .shufflepattern ;reorder StarTrekker patters into FastTracker format
000055E4 E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}   ;R0=start addr of 8 channel pattern to reshuffe
000055E8 E28F2044           ADR     R2,shuffletable     ;(alternate one offset from table, one from R1)
000055EC          
000055EC                    ;if table byte=0, skip, if table byte=255 end
000055EC E2801B01           ADD     R1,R0,#64*16        ;(64 to 127, use inbetween table bytes)
000055F0 E1A03000           MOV     R3,R0               ;output address
000055F4          .shuffleloop 
000055F4 E4D24001           LDRB    R4,[R2],#1          ;load table byte
000055F8 E3340000           TEQ     R4,#0               ;0=skip
000055FC 02833010           ADDEQ   R3,R3,#1*16         ;get to next event position
00005600 0A000006           BEQ     skipthisshuffle
00005604          
00005604          
00005604 E33400FF           TEQ     R4,#255             ;255=exit
00005608 08BD9FFF           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
0000560C          
0000560C          
0000560C E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;load 4 channels from current event
00005610 E0804204           ADD     R4,R0,R4,LSL#4
00005614 E8941E00           LDMIA   R4,{R9,R10,R11,R12} ;load 4 channels from destination event
00005618 E88401E0           STMIA   R4,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
0000561C E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12};swap them round and increment to next event
00005620          .skipthisshuffle 
00005620 E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;...do the next event using R1 as swap address
00005624 E8911E00           LDMIA   R1,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
00005628 E8A101E0           STMIA   R1!,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
0000562C E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
00005630 EAFFFFEF           B       shuffleloop         ;keep going until end of table
00005634          
00005634

00005674                    ; on entry
00005674          
00005674                    ;          R0          \  sample end address            - sample end address
00005674                    ;          R1           } -ve sample offset             - -ve sample offset
00005674                    ;          R2           } (amiga type) pitch            - sample pitch increment (Arc)
00005674                    ;          R3           } linear volume (0-64)          - sample volume (0-255)
00005674                    ;          R4           } -ve repeat offset             - -ve repeat offset
00005674                    ;          R5          /                                - load/store sample byte
00005674                    ;          R6      -ve if channel handler changed       - channel counter (0->channel-1)
00005674                    ;          R7      <unknown>                            -
00005674                    ;          R8      uS <? - unknown>                     - ptr to 'DMA_blocks'
00005674                    ;          R9      ptr to initblock                     - ptr to 'song_data' (usually R12)
00005674                    ;          R10     DMA end                              - DMA end
00005674                    ;          R11     DMA gap                              - DMA gap
00005674                    ;          R12     DMA start                            - DMA ptr
00005674                    ;          R13     stack                                - stack
00005674                    ;          R14     exit                                 - end
00005674

000066B4 00000101           EQUD (1<<8) + 1 + 1*(-1*qtmrast%)  ;rep 1 time, first inst, if qtmrast% add 1 more inst (ADR R14)
000066B8 0000D006           EQUD (dmarep<<8) + 6               ;repeat dmarep times (208 for non-RM QTM), 6 insts
000066BC 00000101           EQUD (1<<8) + 1 + 2*(-1*qtmrast%)  ;rep 1 time, 1 inst, if qtmrast% add 2 more insts (CMP, MOVLT)
000066C0 00000000           EQUD 0
000066C4          
000066C4

000066CC 00000101           EQUD (1<<8) + 1 + 2*(-1*qtmrast%)  ;if qtmrast% this adds 2 more insts (CMP, MOVLT PC,R14)
000066D0 00000000           EQUD 0
